# Context searches for all numbered research notes

Executed: 2026-11-17 20:03:42 UTC.

All 101 items were searched individually with `rg` in the four supplied text files listed below. Fixed-string queries preserve the terms attached to each final note, with hyphen/space variants for the compact dotted terms. The file lists contain matching-line counts and up to four spread-out samples, not every hit. The main notes supply the selected substantive relation and source locator; a lexical hit alone is not evidence for an implication. A zero here is a bounded negative search, not an archive-wide claim.

No manuscript scan was processed by this script. All 48 frozen file hashes were checked before and after.
